- **impeccable-adapt**: Recontextualize an existing web design for a different screen size, device, platform, or use case. Covers assessing what breaks, phone-to-tablet restructuring, orientation and foldables, platform-to-platform idiom translation, and verifying custom controls under real touch input. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-adapt) · ★3)

([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-adapt-native) · ★3)
